8a1(1): gi@#//gnas bzhi pa la rtog par byed de/des thos pa las byung ba'i shes pas pi wang can gsum pa zhes bya ba rnams mthong ngo//de rnams kyi gnas bcu pa 
8a2(1): yang 'di lta ste/dri dga' dang/sgra bsnyan dang de la chags dang/skyor chus skyes dang/lus dga' dang/phan tshun dga' dang/yang dag chags spyod dang/'khor dang rnam spyod
8a3(1): dang /skyil mo krung gi steng du skye ba dang/ldan par gnas pa dag ste/lha pi wang can gsum pa rnams kyi gnas ni bcu po 'di dag yin no//dge slong de de rnams las kyi rnam 
8a4(1): par smin pa rnam par 'byed de/gnas de rnams su lha dag las kyis skye bar 'gyur zhig tu snyam nas/des thos pa las byung pa'i shes pas brtags na/'di ltar las 
8a5(1): de dag byed na/de ltar de dang 'thun par skye bar 'gyur la//las dang 'thun pa rnam par smin par 'gyur ro//de la rgyal chen bzhi'i gnas dang po dri ga zhes bya ba der sems 
8a6(1): can dag las gang gis skye bar 'gyur/snyam nas/des thos pa la sa byung ba'i shes pas brtags na/sems pa dang ldan par gyur cing/des sems bsgoms pa des 
8a7(1): lus bsgoms pa gang gis bsod nams bsags par bya ba'i phyir dge 'dun dag la kun dga' ra ba'i sa 'am/bu ram shing gi ra ba 'am/'bra go'i ra ba 'am//
8a8(1): shing a mra'i ra ba phul la//de yang yongs su spyod par byed na/de lus dang bral nas bde 'gro mtho ris kyis 'jig rten dri dga'i lha rnams kyi nang du skye bar 'gyur ro//der skye
8b1(1): nas kyang bde ba du ma thob cing spyod pa de/lha'i ts+dan dang tsan+dan sa mchog gi byug pas lus byugs pa/lha mos tshogs du mas yongs su bskor ba gzugs dang cha byad sna tshoT 
8b2(1): dang ldan pa dgod pa/dang/rtse ba dang sgeg chos las mkhas pa rnams dang lhan cig pad+ma'i tshal dang/lteng ka dang/nags tshal gyi bar dag na/me tog gi 'phreng ba dang
8b3(1): gos gyon pa yid dga' ba rnams dga' bar byed dga' mgur byed par byed do//de'i lha'i dri rnam pa du ma dag pad+ma'i tshal de dag dang/lha'i me tog de dag brtol 
8b4(1): nas dag+pa chad rgyar gsungs stobs cher 'byung ngo//dri bsnams pa'i lha mo gang yin pa/de dag thams cad dri shin tu mnyams dga' zhing mchog tu yid du 'ong ba rnam
8b5(1): pa du mas myos pa rnams yang lha'i dri des brgya 'gyur du myos par 'gyur ro//shin tu myos pa'i lha'i ris de dag nyid la sems chags pa rnams lha de dag nyid dang/rnam pa 
8b6(1): du ma brgya 'gyur du dga' mgur spyod par byed la/de rnams dang lhan cig chu bo'i drung du 'gro bar byed do//chu bo dag gi ming ni 'di lta ste/rin chen 'bab ces bya 
8b7(1): ba dang/rin po che'i dum bu 'bab dang/gser 'bab dang/chang 'bab chu bo dang/sbrang rtsi 'bab pa'i chu bo dang/b+hu ba rog rog 'bab pa ste/chu bo dge 
8b8(1): thams cad kyi 'bram na/bya dang pa dang/karan+da ba dang/hur pa'i tshogs kyis brgyan pa/bya sgra 'byin pa rnams dang/shing ljon pa'i mtha' khebs pa yin la/de dag
9a1(1): @#//rin po che sna bdun gyi bya rnams kyis kun nas bkrigs shing sgra yid du 'ong ba 'byin pa dag yin te/lha mo'i tshogs glu snyan pa len pa la mkhas pa gang pa 
9a2(1): yin pa de dag ni/bya'i sgra dbyings 'byin pa thos nas tshal gyi nang na rgyu ba'i bya gzhan sgra 'byin pa lha'i lhar snyan pa de dag la shin tu nyams par byed de/yul gyi 'dod pa la 
9a3(1): sems rnam par chags shing bya'i sgra 'byin pa de la/snying shin tu chags pa de dag gis bde ba de thams cad bor nas bya'i sgra de la nyan par byed do//bya'i sgra yid du 'ong ba sna tshoT 
9a4(1): dang /thos na bde ba rnam pa du ma sna tshogs dang sgra dbyangs bdun dang ldan zhing rgyun drag par 'byin pa la nyan par byed do/chu bo gzhan dag dang sgra 'byin par lha mo rnams dang /
9a5(1): dga' bar byed/glu dbyangs len/dgod par byed le/lha'i chang myos pa'i skyon dang bral ba dag la 'thung bar byed do//de dag de rnams kyis mthung nas kyang /de rnams dang lhan 
9a6(1): cig dga' bar byed la baiDUr+ya dang /dngul dang /shel dang /gser gyi ri bo gzhan dag gi rtse mo dang /chu bo dang dang /'ab ba chu dang /lteng ka dang /nags tshal mdzes pa 
9a7(1): dang /pad+ma'i tshal bya'i tshogs sna tshogs kyis brgyan pa rnams su/lha mo de rnams dang lhan cig gcig dga' bar byed do//gzhan yang gzhag phyogs gzhan dag na baiDUr+ya 
9a8(1): sngon po'i dum bu lta bu dang /me tog sna tshogs kyis khe bas pa'i sa phyogs der dag na dga' bar byed de/de ltar de legs par byas pa'i las de dag rgyu mthun pa'i 
9b1(1): sil bsnyan yan lag bdun dang ldan pa'i/mtho ris kyis bde ba yid du 'ong ba'i rnam pa dag myong ngo//lha'i bde ba de yang dge slong des thos pa las byung ba shes 
9b2(1): pa'i brtags n+sa/'di ltar tshigs su bcad de smras pa/rnam par chags pa'i dbang po lnga//de ltar yul rnams lnga la chags//'dod chags skad cig ma 'byung ba 
9b3(1): yul gyi tshim par mi byed do//yul rnams kun gyi nang na cig/'dir ni bud med kho na lta//yul kun zil gyis gnan nas su//'dod chags me ni chen po 'bar/
9b4(1): tshogs pa dang ni ma tshogs dang//'chi ba dang ni gtam brjod pa 'am//bud med rgyu las kun 'byung ste//me yis mi 'di sreg par byed//me ni tshogs dang 'bar
9b5(1): 'gyur te//ma tshogs 'bar bar mi 'gyur la//tshogs na 'ang 'bar la ma tshogs kyang//lha rnams 'dod chags mer 'bar ro//me de'i yul de med pa dang//
9b6(1): rgyang ring na go sreg mi byed//'dod chags mi ni nye ring 'dra nga//mi zad dga' bas sreg par byed//kun tu rtog pa'i shing skam 'byung//dga' ba ya yo'i shugs 
9b7(1): dang ldan//sred pa'i mar la smyugs pa 'di//'dod chags mi yis dga' ba bsreg/'di ltar me'i lus cig ni//bsregs nas rab tu zhi bar 'gyur//mid 
9b8(1): dang gzugs ni ma bral ba'i//'dod chags me ni bzhi mi 'gyur//bsreg byed mi zad me la ni//'byung po dga' bar mi byed la//'dod chags me ni rab rtso ba yang /
10a1(1): @#//mi rnams yid ni byung mi byed//dbang po lnga las byung ba yi//'di ni yul rnams lnga yis bsgribs//sred pa rlung gi stobs dang mtshungs//'dod 
10a2(1): chags me yis skye rgu sreg/rtog pa'i gtsub shing las byung ba'i//yul rnams kyis ni yongs su spel//lus ni shing ltar sreg byed kyang //de ni skye bos mi mthong ngo//
10a3(1): ji lta ji ltar 'dod chags me//rab tu 'bar bas sreg byed pa//de lta de ltar 'dod chags me//bde ba bzhin tu yongs su sten//me ni rab tu gsal 'gyur la//'dod 
10a4(1): chags me ni mun pas bsgribs//de bas dug dang dgra lta bu//brtan pos de dag me spongs shig/de ltar dge slong des de dag mthong nas//'dod chags kyis mes bsreT 
10a5(1): pa'i lha la snying brtse bar byed la//lha'i srid par gtogs pa dag yang skyon de dag rtogs nas sreg par mi byed do//de ltar dri dga'i sa phyogs na bde ba rnam pa du ma dag ji 
10a6(1): srid du 'dod pa sdug pa yid du 'ong ba legs par byas pa'i las de zad par ma gyur pa de srid du dga' bar byed do//de phyis lha'i 'jig rten de nas shi bar 'gyur te/shi n+sa 
10a7(1): kyang gal te lan grangs gzhan la myos par 'gyur ba'i las kyi sems can dmyal ba dang /yi dgas dang /dud 'gro'i nang du ma skyes la/mi rnams dang bskal pa mnyam 
10a8(1): par mi'i nang du skyes nas/de las de dang rgyu mthun par rtag tu sa phyogs bde ba thob cing /shin tu 'byor pa dang ldan par 'gyur/ba mthong ngo//de nas yang dge slong las 
10b1(1): kyi 'bras bu rnam par smin pa shes pa de/lha pi wang can gsum pa rnams kyi gnas la rtog par byed de/des thos pa las byung ba'i shes pas lha rnams kyi gnas sgra bsnyan
10b2(1): pa zhes bya ba ji ltar 'dug pa mthong ngo//der sem can dag las gang gis skye bar 'gyur snyam nas/de thos pa las byung ba'i shesapas brtags na/legs par byas pa'i las
10b3(1): spyod cig/sems pa dang yang dag pa'i lta ba dang ldan par 'gyur na/pa gang gang gis lta ba log par bzhugs pa dag la/tshigs bcad gcig bstan la/de dag kyang sems
10b4(1): yud tsam zhig sangs rgyas la/dad pa skyes na/de lus dang bral nas bde 'gro mtho ris la kyi 'jig rten sgra bsnyan pa'i lha rnams kyi nang du skye bar 'gyur ro//der skyes nas
10b5(1): kyang dbang po yul las dga' zhing bde ba de lha'i mtsho 'gram na/rtse ba la ni dga'/pad+ma'i tshal gyi 'phreng ba dang/gser gyi ri la gnas shing /lha'i rol mo byed pa la ni dga'
10b6(1): lha mo gzugs dang cha byad rnam pa sna tshogs thogs pa 'dzum zhing 'jo sgeg la/mngon par dga' ba/byad bzhin gyi na dangs sdug cig/dbang phyug mtshungs pa med pa la dga'
10b7(1): ba rnams kyis ni/yongs su bskor//rtag tu nyin cig bzhin bde ba la gnas pa rab tu 'phel zhing bai DUr+ya'i ri'i rtse mo dag na gnas la/bde ba sna tshogs spyod pa/me tog gi
10b8(1): 'phreng ba dang/phye ma dang/byug pas/lus brgyan pa dag de na dga' bar byed do//yul gyi rnam pa mang po la dga' ba/ri rab dang/ri khrod yangs pa na gnas pa dag
11a1(1): gi@#//7//gser gyi ri dang tshal gyi rnams kyi nang na dga' bar byed do//de na gser gyi ri 'di lta bu dag yod do//'di lta ste ri kha thug bya ba dang/ri bring
11a2(1): med ces bya ba dang/ri thams cad dga' ba zhes bya ba dang/ri yid bzhin sprul ces bya ba dag ste/ri dag ni rin po che rnam par che zhing mdzes pa la/gser gyi tshal gyis 
11a3(1): mdzes pa'i byas pa'i ri'i rtse mo rnams na/lha'i tshogs snying rab tu dga' ba rnams rtag tu glud byangs kyi sgra ni 'byin/chu dang zhing bsil la gtsang ba'i rgyun dag dang/
11a4(1): pad+ma'i rdzing rnams kyis rtag par mdzes par byas pa/bya rtag tu myos rnar snyan pa sgra 'byin pa rnams ni gnya pa na 'khod/mig ni shin tu sdug pa'i ri phyogs kyi sman ljon 
11a5(1): de rnams na/rang gi las la spyod pa'i lha de dga' bar byed rtse bar byed/rgyu bar byed/dga' mgur spyod par byed do//lha mo bzang mo rnams kyi yongs su bcing/bya myos
11a6(1): pa'i sgra 'byin pa dang/gser gyi bum pa myos pa rnams kyi yongs su bskor te/lha de thams cad reg na bde ba'i reg na bde ba'i lha'i lcag ma'i khyim rnams su 'khor dang lhan cig
11a7(1): dga' bar byed do//de ltar de nas de na ji srid du 'dod pa sdug pa yid du 'ong ba lha las ldog par mi 'gyur ba'i las kyi de zad par ma gyur pa de srid du dga' bar byed do/
11a8(1): gang gi tshe zad par gyur pa dang/de lta'i ris de nas 'chi bar 'gyur te/shi nas kyang la sa nye bar 'gro ba ji lta ba bzhin sems can dmyal ba dang/yi dgas dang//
11b1(1): dud 'gro'i rnams kyi 'khor bar yongs su 'khyam par 'gyur to//gal te mi rnams dang skal ba mnyam par mi'i nang du skye na/de las de dang rgyu 'thun par rtag tu rigs shin
11b2(1): tu che ba'i nang du 'byor pa dang ldan pa dang/skye bo kun gyis mngon pa dang/ris mthun pa'i gzugs bzang po dang ldan pa dang/ris mthun pa'ibaskal pa dang ldan pa dang//
11b3(1): skyon med pa dang/bran po dang/bran mo dang/skye bo dang ldan par 'gyur ba/mthong ngo//de nas yang dge slong las kyi 'bras bu rnam par smin pa shes pa de/lha pi wang can gsu

11b6(1): pa dang/ldan na par gyur pa gang gis grogs po la/skom phor gang tsam blud pa 'am/'dron po rnams la srog chags lu ta dang/ki ta dang/spang bu chu rings dag gis 'jig+sa
11b7(1): pa las bde bar bya ba'i phyir khron pa las dag bgegs par byed de/'di ltar srog chags dug dang/'dres pa 'di dag gi dug chu'i nang du song ste/de las 'dron po rnams
11b8(1): lam du bzhugs pa rnams kyi srog gi bar chad du 'gyur ro//snyam nas/bya ba ci yang med kyis kyis kyang/byams sems kyis 'khron pa/'thungs ba'i skye bo gzhan la
12a1(1): @#//phan par bya ba'i phyir bzhi (de)las g.yog par byed de/skyes bu dge ba de lus dang bral nas bde 'gro mtho ris kyi 'jig rten lha pi wang thogs pa rnams kyi gan(sa)
12a2(1): de la chags pa zhes bya bar 'gyur ro//der skyes nas kyang de'i lus thams cad ni nyi ma gnyis pa ltar gzi che ste/legs par byas pa'i las rnams kyis lus thams cad ni 
12a3(1): brgyan/ri dang ri bo dang/ri sul dang/'a ba ba chu na gnas pa rnams de dang spyod pa'i ngang tshul mthun pa'i lha mo'i tshogs gzugs dang/lang (I)tsho dang/brgyan pa rnam pa sna(sa)

12a7(1): dang bya khu sgra 'byin to//bya de dag kyang rin po che sna bdun gyi sgro gshog dang ldan la sgra snyan cing/'dze bas par 'byin pa yin te/tshal den shin tu nyams dga' bar yang bdag nyid
12a8(1): kyis mthong nas bde bar gyur to//de na bya 'di lta bu dag yod do//bkra ba dang/sgro gshog bkra ba dang/lha rnams kyis snying la 'dod pa bskyed pa gang yin pa de dag des 
12b1(1): sgra 'byin par byed de/sgra ngo thos nas lha rnams kyang ngo mtshar cher skyes nas/'di ltar bya 'di dag ni lha dang mthun par mthong ste/gang bdag cag gi cher skyes nas yid la 'dod pa
12b2(1): dag bya de rnams sgra 'byin par byed do/sgra de thos nas lha rnams kyang bya chu zhim nas zhes bya ba de dag gi kha nas/chang ro dang dri'i ngo bo gtsang ma phun sum tshogs pa 'byung ngo//
12b3(1): kha nas 'byung ba'i chang de la yang /bya gzhan rnams 'thung bar byed do//de 'thungs nas/bya de dag bcu 'gyur du myos shing /yid rab tu dga' nas/brgya 'gyur du sgra 'byin

12b6(1): sgra 'khrol ba'i gas ba gse ba na/dga' bar byed la/'di ltar ci 'di na bya'i sgra 'byin nam/ci dril bu g.yer ka'i sgra 'byin ces gsal bar mi mngon pa de ltar dag dang /lhan

13a1(1): gi @#//du gnas so//bya chags pa zhes bya ba gang gal te lha shin tu nyams dga' ba rnams dang/nye bar lha mo de dag gnas na brgya 'gyur mdzes ste/tshal dang nags
13a2(1): tshal du ma rnams kyi nang na yang rtag tu bde bar gnas pa dag dga' bar byed do//yid la bsams pa (grang )yin pa de dang de dag nyid 'grub par 'gyur te/shin tu brgya che bar 'gyur cing//
13a3(1): dga' ba dang/sdug pa dang/yid du 'ong ba skye'o//lha mo'i tshogs du mas yongs su bskor ba'i lha de/tshal dang/nye ba'i tshal de rnams kyi nang na dga' bar byed de/gser dang

13a7(1): la/kun nas dga' bar byed cing/dga' mgur spyod pa'i byed do//gzugs sdug pa 'bum phrag du ma dag la blta bar byed do//sgra bsnyan pa 'jebs pa yid du 'ong ba 'bum phrag
13a8(1): dum la nyan bar byed do//dri zhim pa drod che ba yid du 'ong ba 'bum phrag du ma dag la snom par byed do//de bzhin reg pas reg par bya ba dag la reg la par byed do//de ltar dbang 
13b1(1): po thams cad yul myong bar byed de/rang gi byas pa sems de ji srid du 'dod pa sdug pa yid du 'ong ba'i las de zad par ma gyur pa de srid du gnas de dag na dga' bar byed do//des

13b6(1): dag las gang gis skye bar 'gyur snyam nas/des thos pa las byung pa'i shes pas brtags na/legs pas spyad pas las kyi mtha' spyad pa/sems cangs yongs su bsgom
13b7(1): pa'i snying rje dang/snying rjes yongs su brnyan pa'i sems dang ldan pa/gang gis sems can 'chi bar 'gyur skom gyis gdungs te/mgul sgra dar dar por 'gags shing/srog cung
13b8(1): zad yod pa la skom blud dam/de bzhin du gang gis de'i srog bde bar 'gyur bar bya ba'i phyir sman dag byin/de'i lus dang bral nas bde 'gro mtho ris 'jig rten pi wang gsum pa'i
14a1(1): @#//lha rnams kyi nang skyor chu skyes pa'i lha rnams kyi nang du skye bar 'gyur ro//der skyes nas kyang de lha'i dbang po'i lus dang mtshungs shing lha mo'i tshogs kyis kun nas 
14a2(1): bskor te/bde ba rgyun mi 'chad pa la rtag tu gnas pa yin no//de ltar rang gi las la spyod pa de tshal du ma dang/chu bo dang 'bab chu du ma dag kyang mthong ste/des 'ga' lha mo'i tshogs kyis
14a3(1): yongs su bskor nas/tshal phreng ba zhes bya ba der 'dod par byed do//tshal de yang chu bo dang/lteng ka dang/pad+ma'i tshal du ma dag gis brgyan cing lha'i sgrol mo dag gis mdzes par byas
14a4(1): la/lha dang lha mo'i tshogs kyi rab tu dga' ba rnams kyis gang ba yin no//tshal de na shing ljon pa me tog dang/'bras bu du mas brgyan pa/lha'i rol mo'i sgra 'byin pa//bya'i sgra ca chos
14a5(1): pa yin no//tshal de ni rin po che sna tshogs kyis shing ljon pa rnams kyis yongs su bskor ba yin te/'di lta ste rtag tu dga' ba dang/yang dag par dga' bar byed pa'i me tog man+da ra ba
14a6(1): dang/pad+ma dang/dri ji ltar dga' ba dang/rnams kyis bskor ba yin no//me tog de dag gi dri'i mthu ni 'di lta bu yin te/lha rnams dri ji lta ji lta 'dod pa de lta de ltar me tog de dag las
